cholesky/cholesky.cpp:11:// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:12:// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:13:
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:14:bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:15:    // A is stored in row-major order
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:16:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:17:        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:18:            double sum = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:19:            
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:20:            if (i == j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:21:                // Diagonal element
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:22:                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:23:                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:24:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:25:                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:26:                if (val <= 0.0) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:27:                    // Matrix is not positive definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:28:                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:29:                    return false;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:30:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:31:                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:32:            } else {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:33:                // Off-diagonal element
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:34:                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:35:                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:36:                }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:37:                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:38:            }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:39:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:40:        
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:41:        // Zero out upper triangular part
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:42:        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:43:            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:44:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:45:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:46:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:47:    return true;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:48:}

cholesky/cholesky.cpp:50:// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:51:void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:52:    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:53:    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:54:    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:55:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:56:    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:57:    unsigned int seed = 42;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:58:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:59:    // Generate random matrix B
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:60:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:61:        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:62:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:63:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:64:    // Compute A = B * B^T
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:65:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:66:        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:67:            double sum = 0.0;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:68:            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:69:                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:70:            }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:71:            A[i * n + j] = sum;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:72:        }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:73:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:74:    
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:75:    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:76:    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:77:        A[i * n + i] += n;
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:78:    }
cholesky/cholesky.cpp:79:}

codex
The timed kernel is a sequential, left-looking scalar factorization. I’m going to replace it with a cache-blocked lower-triangular factorization: diagonal blocks remain dependency-ordered, while panel solves and trailing rank updates run across OpenMP threads inside one persistent parallel region. This reduces synchronization from once per matrix column to once per cache-sized block and exposes most of the cubic work.

+// thread, after which its panel and trailing update are distributed over the
+// team.  Keeping one parallel region alive avoids repeatedly creating a team.
 
 bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    constexpr size_t blockSize = 64;
+    constexpr size_t updateTile = 32;
+    bool positiveDefinite = true;
+    size_t failureIndex = 0;
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(positiveDefinite, failureIndex)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < n; block += blockSize) {
+            const size_t blockEnd = std::min(block + blockSize, n);
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                // Factor the diagonal block. Contributions from earlier
+                // panels have already been removed by their trailing updates.
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double diagonalUpdate = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:diagonalUpdate)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        diagonalUpdate += matrix[j * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+
+                    const double value = matrix[j * n + j] - diagonalUpdate;
+                    if (value <= 0.0) {
+                        positiveDefinite = false;
+                        failureIndex = j;
+                        break;
+                    }
+                    matrix[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+                    for (size_t i = j + 1; i < blockEnd; ++i) {
+                        double update = 0.0;
+                        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                        for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                            update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                        }
+                        matrix[i * n + j] =
+                            (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+
+            // The single construct's implicit barrier publishes the block and
+            // the status flag to every thread.
+            if (!positiveDefinite) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            // Triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block. Rows
+            // are independent, and their panel entries stay hot in cache.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double update = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+                    matrix[i * n + j] =
+                        (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+            }
+
+            // Symmetric rank-k update of the lower trailing matrix. Two-level
+            // tiling both balances the triangular work and reuses panel data.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t rowTile = blockEnd; rowTile < n; rowTile += updateTile) {
+                for (size_t columnTile = blockEnd; columnTile < n; columnTile += updateTile) {
+                    if (columnTile > rowTile) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowTile + updateTile, n);
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(columnTile + updateTile, n);
+                    for (size_t i = rowTile; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        const size_t lastColumn = std::min(columnEnd, i + 1);
+                        for (size_t j = columnTile; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+                            double update = 0.0;
+                            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                            for (size_t p = block; p < blockEnd; ++p) {
+                                update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                            }
+                            matrix[i * n + j] -= update;
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // Match the original in-place representation: only L is retained.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+

+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failureIndex);
+    }
+    return positiveDefinite;

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Cache-blocked Cholesky decomposition.  Each diagonal block is factored by one
+// thread, after which its panel and trailing update are distributed over the
+// team.  Keeping one parallel region alive avoids repeatedly creating a team.
 
 bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    constexpr size_t blockSize = 64;
+    constexpr size_t updateTile = 32;
+    bool positiveDefinite = true;
+    size_t failureIndex = 0;
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(positiveDefinite, failureIndex)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < n; block += blockSize) {
+            const size_t blockEnd = std::min(block + blockSize, n);
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                // Factor the diagonal block. Contributions from earlier
+                // panels have already been removed by their trailing updates.
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double diagonalUpdate = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:diagonalUpdate)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        diagonalUpdate += matrix[j * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+
+                    const double value = matrix[j * n + j] - diagonalUpdate;
+                    if (value <= 0.0) {
+                        positiveDefinite = false;
+                        failureIndex = j;
+                        break;
+                    }
+                    matrix[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+                    for (size_t i = j + 1; i < blockEnd; ++i) {
+                        double update = 0.0;
+                        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                        for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                            update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                        }
+                        matrix[i * n + j] =
+                            (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+
+            // The single construct's implicit barrier publishes the block and
+            // the status flag to every thread.
+            if (!positiveDefinite) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            // Triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block. Rows
+            // are independent, and their panel entries stay hot in cache.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double update = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+                    matrix[i * n + j] =
+                        (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+            }
+
+            // Symmetric rank-k update of the lower trailing matrix. Two-level
+            // tiling both balances the triangular work and reuses panel data.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t rowTile = blockEnd; rowTile < n; rowTile += updateTile) {
+                for (size_t columnTile = blockEnd; columnTile < n; columnTile += updateTile) {
+                    if (columnTile > rowTile) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowTile + updateTile, n);
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(columnTile + updateTile, n);
+                    for (size_t i = rowTile; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        const size_t lastColumn = std::min(columnEnd, i + 1);
+                        for (size_t j = columnTile; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+                            double update = 0.0;
+                            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                            for (size_t p = block; p < blockEnd; ++p) {
+                                update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                            }
+                            matrix[i * n + j] -= update;
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // Match the original in-place representation: only L is retained.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+

+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failureIndex);
+    }

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// Cache-blocked Cholesky decomposition.  Each diagonal block is factored by one
+// thread, after which its panel and trailing update are distributed over the
+// team.  Keeping one parallel region alive avoids repeatedly creating a team.
 
 bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    constexpr size_t blockSize = 64;
+    constexpr size_t updateTile = 32;
+    bool positiveDefinite = true;
+    size_t failureIndex = 0;
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(positiveDefinite, failureIndex)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < n; block += blockSize) {
+            const size_t blockEnd = std::min(block + blockSize, n);
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                // Factor the diagonal block. Contributions from earlier
+                // panels have already been removed by their trailing updates.
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double diagonalUpdate = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:diagonalUpdate)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        diagonalUpdate += matrix[j * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+
+                    const double value = matrix[j * n + j] - diagonalUpdate;
+                    if (value <= 0.0) {
+                        positiveDefinite = false;
+                        failureIndex = j;
+                        break;
+                    }
+                    matrix[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+                    for (size_t i = j + 1; i < blockEnd; ++i) {
+                        double update = 0.0;
+                        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                        for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                            update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                        }
+                        matrix[i * n + j] =
+                            (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+
+            // The single construct's implicit barrier publishes the block and
+            // the status flag to every thread.
+            if (!positiveDefinite) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            // Triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block. Rows
+            // are independent, and their panel entries stay hot in cache.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double update = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+                    matrix[i * n + j] =
+                        (matrix[i * n + j] - update) / matrix[j * n + j];
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+            }
+
+            // Symmetric rank-k update of the lower trailing matrix. Two-level
+            // tiling both balances the triangular work and reuses panel data.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t rowTile = blockEnd; rowTile < n; rowTile += updateTile) {
+                for (size_t columnTile = blockEnd; columnTile < n; columnTile += updateTile) {
+                    if (columnTile > rowTile) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowTile + updateTile, n);
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(columnTile + updateTile, n);
+                    for (size_t i = rowTile; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        const size_t lastColumn = std::min(columnEnd, i + 1);
+                        for (size_t j = columnTile; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+                            double update = 0.0;
+                            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                            for (size_t p = block; p < blockEnd; ++p) {
+                                update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                            }
+                            matrix[i * n + j] -= update;
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // Match the original in-place representation: only L is retained.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    if (!positiveDefinite) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failureIndex);
+    }
+    return positiveDefinite;
 }

Correctness now passes sizes on both sides of every cache-block boundary tested, and the rebuilt executable is linked to the OpenMP runtime. At 512×512 with four threads, the timed factorization dropped from the original 28 ms to 7 ms; I’m running larger matrices now because those give a more reliable scalability signal than millisecond-scale runs.

-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {

+    constexpr size_t blockSize = 64;
+    constexpr size_t updateTile = 32;
+    bool positiveDefinite = true;
+    size_t failureIndex = 0;
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(positiveDefinite, failureIndex)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < n; block += blockSize) {
+            const size_t blockEnd = std::min(block + blockSize, n);
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                // Factor the diagonal block. Contributions from earlier
+                // panels have already been removed by their trailing updates.
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double diagonalUpdate = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:diagonalUpdate)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        diagonalUpdate += matrix[j * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+
+                    const double value = matrix[j * n + j] - diagonalUpdate;
+                    if (value <= 0.0) {
+                        positiveDefinite = false;
+                        failureIndex = j;
+                        break;
+                    }
+                    matrix[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+                    for (size_t i = j + 1; i < blockEnd; ++i) {
+                        double update = 0.0;
+                        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                        for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                            update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                        }
+                        matrix[i * n + j] =

+                    }

-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+
+            // The single construct's implicit barrier publishes the block and
+            // the status flag to every thread.
+            if (!positiveDefinite) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            // Triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block. Rows
+            // are independent, and their panel entries stay hot in cache.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = block; j < blockEnd; ++j) {
+                    double update = 0.0;
+                    #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                    for (size_t p = block; p < j; ++p) {
+                        update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                    }
+                    matrix[i * n + j] =

+            // tiling both balances the triangular work and reuses panel data.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t rowTile = blockEnd; rowTile < n; rowTile += updateTile) {
+                for (size_t columnTile = blockEnd; columnTile < n; columnTile += updateTile) {
+                    if (columnTile > rowTile) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowTile + updateTile, n);
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(columnTile + updateTile, n);
+                    for (size_t i = rowTile; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        const size_t lastColumn = std::min(columnEnd, i + 1);
+                        for (size_t j = columnTile; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+                            double update = 0.0;
+                            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:update)
+                            for (size_t p = block; p < blockEnd; ++p) {
+                                update += matrix[i * n + p] * matrix[j * n + p];
+                            }
+                            matrix[i * n + j] -= update;
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // Match the original in-place representation: only L is retained.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

     }
-    
-    return true;

+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failureIndex);
+    }

Scaling remains strong on the available dual-socket EPYC: for 2048×2048, the factorization moved from 624 ms at one thread to 27 ms at 64 threads and 21 ms at 128 threads, while validation still passes. I also removed duplicate work in positive-definite matrix construction by computing one symmetric triangle and mirroring it, without changing the generated values.

- Builds cleanly with GCC/OpenMP.
